```python
"""
Optimizer for minimizing 2n*max(conv(a,a))/sum(a)^2 over nonnegative a.

Strategy:
- Work with sequences normalized to sum 1.
- Use fast FFT convolution and correlations.
- Main workhorse is an alternating method that:
  * picks active subgradients, using nonnegative least-squares to approximate KKT
    weights on the currently important peaks;
  * applies multiplicative (log-domain) scaling toward a flat weighted gradient;
  * occasionally projects/squares toward a flattened convolution profile.
- Add L-BFGS on softmax variables for local polishing.
- Use random perturbations and quick refinements to escape symmetric stationary points.
- Return the best verified sequence found before the time budget.
"""

import numpy as np
import math
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize, nnls

TARGET_SCORE = 1.5027
MAX_N_OPT = 12000


def _next_power2(m):
    p = 1
    while p < m:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def _normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.zeros_like(a, dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s


def _softmax_from_y(y):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if y.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(y)):
        y = np.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    y = y - float(np.max(y))
    e = np.exp(y)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(y.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(y.size)
    return e / s


def _convolution_fft(a, M=None):
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if M is None:
        M = _next_power2(2 * n - 1)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, M)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, M)
    c = c[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    s = float(c.sum())
    if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
        c = c / s
    return c


def exact_score(a, M=None):
    a = _normalize(a)
    if a.size == 0 or float(a.sum()) <= 0.0:
        return float("inf")
    n = int(a.size)
    if M is None:
        M = _next_power2(2 * n - 1)
    c = _convolution_fft(a, M)
    if c.size == 0 or float(c.sum()) <= 0.0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))


def _cross_corr(a, p, M):
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    PA = np.fft.rfft(p, M)
    Arev = np.fft.rfft(a[n - 1::-1], M)
    h = np.fft.irfft(Arev * PA, M)
    h = h[:2 * n - 1]
    h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
    return h[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def _lse_weights(c, beta):
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if beta <= 0.0 or c.size == 0:
        w = np.zeros_like(c)
        if c.size:
            w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return cmax, w
    scaled = beta * (c - cmax)
    scaled = np.clip(scaled, -500.0, 500.0)
    ex = np.exp(scaled)
    s = float(ex.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        w = np.zeros_like(c)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return cmax, w
    w = ex / s
    L = cmax + math.log(s) / float(beta)
    return L, w


def _top_weights(c, k):
    k = int(min(max(1, k), c.size))
    idx = np.argsort(c)[::-1][:k]
    w = np.zeros_like(c)
    w[idx] = 1.0 / float(k)
    return float(np.max(c)), w


def _kkt_weights(a, c, n, top_k=48, rng=None):
    """
    Approximate a KKT subgradient weight vector on a set of active peaks.

    We choose top peaks and solve a nonnegative least-squares problem
    T p ~= 1 on the support of a, where (T p)_j = sum_l a_l p_{j+l}.
    The direction of p is the relevant subgradient direction; we then
    normalize p to sum to one.
    """
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = int(a.size)
    m = int(c.size)
    if n <= 0 or m <= 0 or float(a.sum()) <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros(m)
        if m:
            w[0] = 1.0
        return w

    top_k = int(max(8, min(64, top_k)))
    if n > 10000:
        top_k = min(top_k, 32)

    idx = np.argsort(c)[::-1][:top_k]
    cmax = float(np.max(c))

    # Include peaks very close to the maximum, but keep the set bounded.
    if cmax > 0.0:
        tol = max(1e-14, 1e-4 * cmax)
        close = np.where(c >= cmax - tol)[0]
        if close.size > top_k:
            close_vals = c[close]
            order = np.argsort(close_vals)[::-1][:top_k]
            idx = close[order]
        elif close.size >= 2:
            idx = np.unique(idx.astype(np.int64) * 0 + close)

    idx = np.asarray(idx, dtype=np.int64)
    if idx.size == 0:
        w = np.zeros(m)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return w

    mask = a > 1e-8
    js = np.where(mask)[0]
    if js.size == 0:
        # Fall back to the largest peaks.
        w = np.zeros_like(c)
        w[idx] = 1.0 / float(idx.size)
        return w

    T = np.zeros((js.size, idx.size), dtype=np.float64)
    for col, k in enumerate(idx):
        k = int(k)
        j0 = max(0, k - n + 1)
        j1 = min(k, n - 1)
        if j1 < j0:
            continue
        # For j in [j0, j1], l = k - j runs from k-j0 down to k-j1.
        vals = a[k - j1:k - j0 + 1][::-1]
        full_col = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        full_col[j0:j1 + 1] = vals
        T[:, col] = full_col[mask]

    y = np.ones(js.size, dtype=np.float64)
    try:
        p_raw, _ = nnls(T, y)
        p_raw = np.where(np.isfinite(p_raw), p_raw, 0.0)
        p_raw = np.maximum(p_raw, 0.0)
        s = float(p_raw.sum())
        if s <= 1e-15 or not np.isfinite(s):
            raise ValueError("bad nnls")
        p = p_raw / s
    except Exception:
        p = np.ones(idx.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(idx.size)

    # Break symmetric stationary points by randomizing the chosen active-set
    # direction slightly.
    if rng is not None and p.size > 1:
        try:
            noise = np.exp(0.08 * rng.standard_normal(p.size))
            p *= noise
            s = float(p.sum())
            if s > 1e-15 and np.isfinite(s):
                p /= s
        except Exception:
            pass

    w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    w[idx] = p
    wsum = float(w.sum())
    if wsum <= 0.0:
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        w = w / wsum
    return w


def _make_target_c(c, cap, smooth=True):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if c.size == 0:
        return c
    s = float(c.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return c
    c = c / s
    cap = float(cap)
    if not np.isfinite(cap) or cap <= 0.0:
        return c
    if cap >= float(np.max(c)):
        return c

    new = np.minimum(c, cap)
    sumnew = float(new.sum())
    if sumnew <= 0.0:
        return c

    deficit = 1.0 - sumnew
    if deficit > 0.0:
        space = np.maximum(cap - new, 0.0)
        spsum = float(space.sum())
        if spsum > 1e-30:
            scale = deficit / spsum
            if scale <= 1.0:
                new = new + scale * space
            else:
                new = new / sumnew
        else:
            new = new / sumnew

    new = np.where(np.isfinite(new), new, 0.0)
    new = np.maximum(new, 0.0)
    ns = float(new.sum())
    if ns <= 0.0:
        return c
    new = new / ns

    if smooth and new.size > 16:
        ks = np.arange(-3, 4, dtype=np.float64)
        ker = np.exp(-0.5 * (ks / 2.0) ** 2)
        ker_sum = float(ker.sum())
        if ker_sum > 0.0:
            ker = ker / ker_sum
            tmp = np.convolve(new, ker, mode="same")
            tmp = np.where(np.isfinite(tmp), tmp, 0.0)
            new = 0.8 * new + 0.2 * tmp
            ns2 = float(new.sum())
            if ns2 > 0.0:
                new = new / ns2

    return new


def _c_projection_step(a, c_target, M, n, strength):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    c_target = np.asarray(c_target, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    strength = float(np.clip(strength, 0.02, 1.0))
    if a.size == 0:
        return a

    A = np.fft.rfft(a, M)
    C = A * A
    Ct = np.fft.rfft(c_target, M)

    ratio = np.ones_like(C, dtype=np.complex128)
    mask = np.abs(C) > 1e-20
    ratio[mask] = Ct[mask] / C[mask]

    mag = np.abs(ratio)
    mag = np.where(mag > 0.0, mag, 1e-4)
    mag = np.clip(mag, 1e-4, 1e4)
    pha = np.angle(ratio)
    pha = np.where(np.isfinite(pha), pha, 0.0)

    logfac = 0.5 * strength * (np.log(mag) + 1j * pha)
    factor = np.exp(logfac)

    # Keep DC stable.
    if C.size > 0 and np.abs(C[0].real) > 1e-30 and Ct[0].real >= 0.0:
        dc = np.abs(Ct[0].real) / np.abs(C[0].real)
        dc = float(np.clip(dc, 1e-4, 1e4))
        factor[0] = dc ** (0.5 * strength)

    Anew = A * factor
    a_new = np.fft.irfft(Anew, M)[:n]
    a_new = np.where(np.isfinite(a_new), a_new, 0.0)
    return np.maximum(a_new, 0.0)


def _softmax_obj(y, beta, n, M, reg):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if y.size != n:
        return 1e12, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(y)):
        y = np.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)

    m_orig = float(np.mean(y))
    yc = y - m_orig
    a = _softmax_from_y(yc)
    c = _convolution_fft(a, M)
    L, w = _lse_weights(c, beta)

    h = _cross_corr(a, w, M)
    D = 2.0 * h
    meanD = float(np.dot(a, D))
    tangent = D - meanD
    grad = 2.0 * float(n) * a * tangent

    if reg > 0.0:
        grad = grad + reg * m_orig

    val = 2.0 * float(n) * L + 0.5 * reg * m_orig * m_orig
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        val = 1e12
    grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
    return float(val), grad


def run_lbfgs(a, beta, maxiter, maxfun, M, n):
    a = _normalize(a)
    if a.size == 0 or float(a.sum()) <= 0.0:
        return a, float("inf")

    a_safe = np.maximum(a, 1e-15)
    a_safe = a_safe / float(a_safe.sum())
    y = np.log(a_safe)
    y = y - float(np.mean(y))

    reg = 1e-10
    maxiter = int(max(50, maxiter))
    maxfun = int(max(200, maxfun, 2 * maxiter))

    try:
        res = minimize(
            _softmax_obj,
            y,
            args=(float(beta), int(n), int(M), reg),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            options={
                "maxiter": maxiter,
                "maxfun": maxfun,
                "ftol": 1e-14,
                "gtol": 1e-10,
                "maxls": 30,
                "disp": False,
            },
        )
        x = res.x
        a_out = _softmax_from_y(x)
    except Exception:
        a_out = a_safe

    sc = exact_score(a_out, M)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        a_out = a
        sc = exact_score(a, M)
    return a_out, sc


def _multiplicative_scaling_step(a, w, M, n, eta):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    h = _cross_corr(a, w, M)
    h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
    h = np.maximum(h, 1e-30)

    logh = np.log(h)
    logh = np.where(np.isfinite(logh), logh, 0.0)
    logmean = float(np.dot(a, logh))
    if not np.isfinite(logmean):
        logmean = 0.0

    logstep = -float(eta) * (logh - logmean)
    logstep = np.clip(logstep, -2.5, 2.5)
    a_new = a * np.exp(logstep)
    return _normalize(a_new)


def run_alternating(a, M, n, target_score, deadline,
                    beta=500.0, eta=0.30, strength=0.25,
                    inner=2, c_every=4, kkt_every=3, kkt_top=48, rng=None):
    a = _normalize(a)
    if a.size == 0 or float(a.sum()) <= 0.0:
        return a, float("inf")
    if not np.isfinite(target_score) or target_score <= 0.0:
        target_score = 1.50

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score(a, M)

    beta_cur = float(beta)
    eta_cur = float(eta)
    strength_cur = float(strength)
    it = 0

    while True:
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        if it > 10000000:
            break

        c = _convolution_fft(a, M)
        Mcur = float(np.max(c))
        sc = float(2.0 * n * Mcur)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()

        # Choose subgradient weights.
        if kkt_every > 0 and (it % kkt_every) == kkt_every - 1:
            try:
                w = _kkt_weights(a, c, n, top_k=kkt_top, rng=rng)
            except Exception:
                _, w = _lse_weights(c, beta_cur)
        else:
            _, w = _lse_weights(c, beta_cur)

        old_a = a.copy()
        old_sc = sc

        for _ in range(max(1, int(inner))):
            a_new = _multiplicative_scaling_step(a, w, M, n, eta_cur)
            if float(a_new.sum()) <= 0.0:
                break
            a = a_new

        sc_new = exact_score(a, M)
        admitted = np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < old_sc
        if admitted:
            if sc_new < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc_new
                best_a = a.copy()
            sc = sc_new
            eta_cur = min(0.85, eta_cur * 1.02)
        else:
            a = old_a
            sc = old_sc
            eta_cur = max(0.04, eta_cur * 0.80)

        # Occasional convex-spectrum projection toward a flattened convolution.
        if c_every > 0 and (it % c_every) == c_every - 1 and Mcur > 0.0:
            c2 = _convolution_fft(a, M)
            M2 = float(np.max(c2))
            sc = float(2.0 * n * M2)
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = a.copy()

            target_M = float(target_score) / (2.0 * float(n))
            if target_M <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(target_M):
                target_M = M2 * 0.99

            if M2 <= target_M * 1.02:
                cap = M2 * 0.99
            else:
                cap = math.sqrt(M2 * (target_M * 1.03))
                if (not np.isfinite(cap)) or cap <= 0.0:
                    cap = M2 * 0.5
                if cap >= M2:
                    cap = M2 * 0.95

            try:
                ct = _make_target_c(c2, cap, smooth=True)
                ap = _c_projection_step(a, ct, M, n, strength_cur)
                ap = _normalize(ap)
                if float(ap.sum()) > 0.0:
                    a_try = 0.5 * a + 0.5 * ap
                    a_try = _normalize(a_try)
                    sc_try = exact_score(a_try, M)
                    if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < sc:
                        a = a_try
                        sc = sc_try
                        if sc < best_sc:
                            best_sc = sc
                            best_a = a.copy()
                        strength_cur = min(0.70, strength_cur * 1.05)
                    else:
                        strength_cur = max(0.10, strength_cur * 0.92)
            except Exception:
                pass

        if it % 20 == 0:
            beta_cur = min(200000.0, beta_cur * 1.25)

        it += 1

    return best_a, best_sc


def run_scaling(a, M, n, eta=0.35, max_outer=100000, inner=2,
                beta=1000.0, beta_max=200000.0, top_every=0, top_k=20,
                kkt_every=0, kkt_top=48, deadline=None, rng=None):
    a = _normalize(a)
    if a.size == 0 or float(a.sum()) <= 0.0:
        return a, float("inf")

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score(a, M)

    eta_cur = float(eta)
    beta_cur = float(beta)
    fails = 0
    it = 0

    while it < int(max_outer):
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

        c = _convolution_fft(a, M)
        cmax = float(np.max(c))
        sc = float(2.0 * n * cmax)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()

        # Weight choice.
        used_kkt = False
        if kkt_every > 0 and (it % kkt_every) == kkt_every - 1:
            try:
                w = _kkt_weights(a, c, n, top_k=kkt_top, rng=rng)
                used_kkt = True
            except Exception:
                used_kkt = False
        if not used_kkt:
            if top_every > 0 and (it % top_every) == top_every - 1:
                _, w = _top_weights(c, top_k)
            else:
                _, w = _lse_weights(c, beta_cur)

        old_a = a.copy()
        old_sc = sc

        for _ in range(max(1, int(inner))):
            a_new = _multiplicative_scaling_step(a, w, M, n, eta_cur)
            if float(a_new.sum()) <= 0.0:
                break
            a = a_new

        sc_new = exact_score(a, M)
        if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < old_sc:
            if sc_new < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc_new
                best_a = a.copy()
            eta_cur = min(0.90, eta_cur * 1.03)
            fails = 0
            beta_cur = min(float(beta_max), beta_cur * 1.08)
        else:
            a = old_a
            eta_cur = max(0.02, eta_cur * 0.75)
            fails += 1
            if fails > 8:
                beta_cur = max(50.0, beta_cur * 0.85)
                fails = 0

        it += 1

    return best_a, best_sc


def run_cspace(a, M, n, target_score, max_iters=100000,
               strength0=0.25, strength_max=0.65, deadline=None):
    a = _normalize(a)
    if a.size == 0 or float(a.sum()) <= 0.0:
        return a, float("inf")
    if not np.isfinite(target_score) or target_score <= 0.0:
        target_score = 1.50

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score(a, M)
    strength = float(strength0)

    for it in range(int(max_iters)):
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

        c = _convolution_fft(a, M)
        Mcur = float(np.max(c))
        sc = float(2.0 * n * Mcur)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()
        if Mcur <= 1e-15:
            break

        target_M = float(target_score) / (2.0 * float(n))
        if target_M <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(target_M):
            target_M = Mcur * 0.99

        if Mcur <= target_M * 1.02:
            cap = Mcur * 0.99
        else:
            cap = math.sqrt(Mcur * (target_M * 1.03))
            if (not np.isfinite(cap)) or cap <= 0.0:
                cap = Mcur * 0.5
            if cap >= Mcur:
                cap = Mcur * 0.95

        try:
            ct = _make_target_c(c, cap, smooth=True)
            ap = _c_projection_step(a, ct, M, n, strength)
            ap = _normalize(ap)
            if float(ap.sum()) <= 0.0:
                continue
            blend = min(0.85, 0.30 + 0.50 * strength)
            a_new = (1.0 - blend) * a + blend * ap
            a_new = _normalize(a_new)
            sc_new = exact_score(a_new, M)
            if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < sc:
                a = a_new
                if sc_new < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc_new
                    best_a = a.copy()
                strength = min(float(strength_max), strength * 1.04)
            else:
                strength = max(0.08, strength * 0.90)
        except Exception:
            strength = max(0.10, strength * 0.95)

    return best_a, best_sc


def _resample(a, new_n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    old_n = int(a.size)
    new_n = int(new_n)
    if old_n <= 0 or new_n <= 0:
        return a
    if old_n == new_n:
        return a.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    b = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    return _normalize(b)


def _smooth(a, sigma):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size < 5:
        return a
    sigma = float(max(0.5, sigma))
    r = int(max(1, 3.0 * sigma))
    x = np.arange(-r, r + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    ker = np.exp(-0.5 * (x / sigma) ** 2)
    s = float(ker.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return a
    ker = ker / s
    return np.convolve(a, ker, mode="same")


def _perturb(a, rng, kind):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1).copy()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 1:
        return a
    kind = int(kind) % 7

    if kind == 0:
        a *= np.exp(0.03 * rng.standard_normal(n))
    elif kind == 1:
        a *= np.exp(0.06 * rng.standard_normal(n))
        a = _smooth(a, max(1.0, n / 300.0))
    elif kind == 2:
        x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(max(1, n - 1))
        freq = int(rng.integers(1, 10))
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        a *= 1.0 + 0.08 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * freq * x + phase)
    elif kind == 3:
        bs = max(1, n // 40)
        for _ in range(6):
            s = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - bs)))
            a[s:s + bs] *= np.exp(0.12 * float(rng.standard_normal()))
    elif kind == 4:
        mask = rng.random(n) < 0.03
        a[mask] = 0.0
    elif kind == 5:
        sig = max(1.0, float(rng.integers(1, max(2, n // 100) + 1)))
        a = _smooth(a, sig)
    else:
        a += 0.005 * rng.random(n)

    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    else:
        a = a / s
    return a


def _generate_shapes(n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return []
    j = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    denom = max(1.0, float(n - 1))
    cand = []
    cand.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
    cand.append(j + 1.0)
    cand.append((n - 1.0 - j) + 1.0)
    cand.append(np.minimum(j + 1.0, n - j))
    for alpha in (0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
        cand.append((j + 1.0) ** alpha)
        cand.append((n - j) ** alpha)
    cand.append(1.0 + 0.5 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * j / denom))
    cand.append(1.0 + 0.8 * np.cos(np.pi * j / denom))
    cand.append(np.sin(np.pi * j / denom) + 1e-6)
    center = 0.5 * (n - 1.0)
    sigma = max(1.0, n / 8.0)
    cand.append(np.exp(-((j - center) ** 2) / (2.0 * sigma * sigma)))
    try:
        cand.append(np.maximum(rng.random(n), 1e-3))
    except Exception:
        pass

    out = []
    for c in cand:
        a = _normalize(c)
        if a.size == n and float(a.sum()) > 0.0:
            out.append(a)
    return out


def _pool_add(pool, a, sc):
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return pool
    pool.append((float(sc), np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()))
    if len(pool) > 12:
        max_idx = 0
        for i in range(1, len(pool)):
            if pool[i][0] > pool[max_idx][0]:
                max_idx = i
        del pool[max_idx]
    return pool


def _make_output(a):
    a = _normalize(a)
    out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in a]
    if not out:
        out = [0.5] * 64
    if sum(out) <= 0.01:
        out = [0.5] * 64
    return out


def stochastic_search(a, M, n, target_score, deadline, rng):
    a = _normalize(a)
    if a.size == 0 or float(a.sum()) <= 0.0:
        return a, float("inf")
    if not np.isfinite(target_score) or target_score <= 0.0:
        target_score = 1.50

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score(a, M)
    attempts = 0

    while attempts < 6:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if deadline is not None and now >= deadline:
            break
        remaining = (deadline - now) if deadline is not None else 60.0
        if remaining < 25.0:
            break

        kind = int(rng.integers(0, 7))
        ap = _perturb(best_a, rng, kind)
        slice_deadline = now + min(75.0, max(20.0, 0.18 * remaining))
        if deadline is not None:
            slice_deadline = min(slice_deadline, deadline)

        try:
            if attempts % 3 == 0:
                ao, so = run_alternating(
                    ap, M, n, target_score, slice_deadline,
                    beta=1000.0, eta=0.35, strength=0.30,
                    inner=2, c_every=0, kkt_every=3, kkt_top=48, rng=rng
                )
            elif attempts % 3 == 1:
                ao, so = run_scaling(
                    ap, M, n, eta=0.35, max_outer=100000, inner=2,
                    beta=2000.0, kkt_every=4, kkt_top=48,
                    deadline=slice_deadline, rng=rng
                )
            else:
                ao, so = run_cspace(
                    ap, M, n, target_score, max_iters=100000,
                    strength0=0.25, strength_max=0.55, deadline=slice_deadline
                )
            if np.isfinite(so) and so < best_sc:
                best_sc = so
                best_a = ao.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass

        attempts += 1

    return best_a, best_sc


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = float(budget_s)
    reserve = min(240.0, max(30.0, 0.10 * total_budget))
    deadline = t_start + max(1.0, total_budget - reserve)

    parent = None
    try:
        p = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if p is not None:
            p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
            if p.size >= 16:
                parent = _normalize(p)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    pool = []
    best_a = None
    best_sc = float("inf")

    # Initial candidate.
    if parent is not None:
        a0 = parent.copy()
        if a0.size < 128:
            a0 = _resample(a0, 2048)
        if a0.size > MAX_N_OPT:
            a0 = _resample(a0, min(MAX_N_OPT, 8192))
    else:
        a0 = np.ones(4096, dtype=np.float64)

    a0 = _normalize(a0)
    sc0 = exact_score(a0)
    best_a, best_sc = a0.copy(), sc0
    pool = _pool_add(pool, best_a, best_sc)
    print(f"Initial candidate: n={a0.size}, internal_score={sc0:.8f}")

    # Length variations from the initial candidate.
    base_n = int(a0.size)
    length_set = {base_n}
    for L in (int(base_n * 0.75), int(base_n * 1.25), 1024, 2048, 4096, 8192):
        if 256 <= L <= MAX_N_OPT:
            length_set.add(int(L))

    for L in sorted(length_set):
        if L == base_n:
            continue
        ar = _resample(a0, L)
        sr = exact_score(ar)
        pool = _pool_add(pool, ar, sr)
        if np.isfinite(sr) and sr < best_sc:
            best_sc = sr
            best_a = ar.copy()
            print(f" Length {L}: internal_score={sr:.8f}")

    # Shape candidates at the current best length.
    n0 = int(best_a.size)
    M0 = _next_power2(2 * n0 - 1)
    for sh in _generate_shapes(n0, rng):
        ss = exact_score(sh, M0)
        pool = _pool_add(pool, sh, ss)
        if np.isfinite(ss) and ss < best_sc:
            best_sc = ss
            best_a = sh.copy()
            print(f" Better shape: internal_score={ss:.8f}")

    target_c = min(1.5010, best_sc - 1e-4)
    if not np.isfinite(target_c) or target_c <= 1.0:
        target_c = best_sc * 0.999 if np.isfinite(best_sc) else 1.50
    target_c = max(1.001, target_c)

    usable = max(1.0, deadline - t_start)

    # Phase 1: main alternating KKT + scaling + c-projection.
    ph1 = t_start + 0.45 * usable
    try:
        a1, sc1 = run_alternating(
            best_a, M0, n0, target_c, ph1,
            beta=500.0, eta=0.30, strength=0.25,
            inner=2, c_every=4, kkt_every=3, kkt_top=56, rng=rng
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc1) and sc1 < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc1
            best_a = a1.copy()
        pool = _pool_add(pool, a1, sc1)
    except Exception as e:
        print("Phase 1 alternating failed:", repr(e))

    # Phase 2: pure scaling with occasional KKT weights.
    ph2 = t_start + 0.62 * usable
    try:
        a2, sc2 = run_scaling(
            best_a, M0, n0, eta=0.35, max_outer=100000, inner=2,
            beta=2000.0, top_every=0, top_k=0,
            kkt_every=4, kkt_top=56, deadline=ph2, rng=rng
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc2
            best_a = a2.copy()
        pool = _pool_add(pool, a2, sc2)
    except Exception as e:
        print("Phase 2 scaling failed:", repr(e))

    # Phase 3: L-BFGS polishing with a couple of smoothness levels.
    ph3 = t_start + 0.82 * usable
    for beta in (1000.0, 5000.0, 20000.0, 80000.0):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now > ph3 - 10.0:
            break
        remaining = max(1.0, ph3 - now - 5.0)
        maxiter = int(min(3000, max(150, 0.7 * remaining)))
        maxfun = int(max(1000, 25 * maxiter))
        try:
            a3, sc3 = run_lbfgs(best_a, beta, maxiter, maxfun, M0, n0)
            if np.isfinite(sc3) and sc3 < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc3
                best_a = a3.copy()
            pool = _pool_add(pool, a3, sc3)
        except Exception as e:
            print("L-BFGS failed beta", beta, repr(e))
            break

        # Quick scaling polish after each L-BFGS run if time remains.
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now < ph3 - 15.0:
            try:
                a4, sc4 = run_scaling(
                    best_a, M0, n0, eta=0.20, max_outer=2000, inner=2,
                    beta=max(1000.0, beta * 2.0),
                    kkt_every=3, kkt_top=48,
                    deadline=min(ph3, now + 20.0), rng=rng
                )
                if np.isfinite(sc4) and sc4 < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc4
                    best_a = a4.copy()
                pool = _pool_add(pool, a4, sc4)
            except Exception as e:
                print("Quick polish failed:", repr(e))

    # Phase 4: c-space projection.
    ph4 = t_start + 0.92 * usable
    try:
        a5, sc5 = run_cspace(
            best_a, M0, n0, target_c, max_iters=100000,
            strength0=0.25, strength_max=0.60, deadline=ph4
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc5) and sc5 < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc5
            best_a = a5.copy()
        pool = _pool_add(pool, a5, sc5)
    except Exception as e:
        print("Phase 4 cspace failed:", repr(e))

    # Phase 5: stochastic restarts.
    try:
        a6, sc6 = stochastic_search(
            best_a, M0, n0, target_c,
            max(deadline - 45.0, t_start), rng
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc6) and sc6 < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc6
            best_a = a6.copy()
        pool = _pool_add(pool, a6, sc6)
    except Exception as e:
        print("Stochastic search failed:", repr(e))

    # Final polish.
    now = time.monotonic()
    if now < deadline - 20.0:
        try:
            a7, sc7 = run_alternating(
                best_a, M0, n0, target_c, deadline - 20.0,
                beta=5000.0, eta=0.20, strength=0.20,
                inner=2, c_every=2, kkt_every=2, kkt_top=48, rng=rng
            )
            if np.isfinite(sc7) and sc7 < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc7
                best_a = a7.copy()
            pool = _pool_add(pool, a7, sc7)
        except Exception as e:
            print("Final alternating failed:", repr(e))

    now = time.monotonic()
    if now < deadline - 30.0:
        try:
            maxiter = int(min(2500, max(100, 0.5 * (deadline - now - 20.0))))
            maxfun = int(max(800, 25 * maxiter))
            a8, sc8 = run_lbfgs(best_a, 100000.0, maxiter, maxfun, M0, n0)
            if np.isfinite(sc8) and sc8 < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc8
                best_a = a8.copy()
            pool = _pool_add(pool, a8, sc8)
        except Exception as e:
            print("Final L-BFGS failed:", repr(e))

    # Choose final output. Prefer the internal best, but verify a couple of
    # candidates if cheap enough to do so.
    out = _make_output(best_a)
    verified_best = float("inf")
    verified_out = out

    try:
        if len(out) <= 10000:
            v = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
            if np.isfinite(v):
                verified_best = v
                verified_out = out
    except Exception:
        verified_best = float("inf")
        verified_out = out

    # Verify a small number of alternative candidates if available.
    if len(out) <= 10000 and len(pool) > 1:
        scored = []
        for s, aa in pool:
            scored.append((float(s), int(aa.size), np.asarray(aa, dtype=np.float64).copy()))
        # Simple selection sort by first component.
        for i in range(len(scored)):
            for j in range(i + 1, len(scored)):
                if scored[j][0] < scored[i][0]:
                    scored[i], scored[j] = scored[j], scored[i]
        check_count = 0
        for s, ln, aa in scored:
            if check_count >= 2:
                break
            if ln > 10000:
                continue
            oo = _make_output(aa)
            try:
                vv = float(evaluate_sequence(oo))
                if np.isfinite(vv) and vv < verified_best:
                    verified_best = vv
                    verified_out = oo
            except Exception:
                pass
            check_count += 1

    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_start
    print(f"Final internal score: {best_sc:.8f}")
    print(f"Final verified score: {verified_best:.8f}")
    print(f"Final length: {len(verified_out)}, elapsed: {elapsed:.1f}s")

    return verified_out
```
